- **2026-08-30** — (worktree branch `claude/relaxed-hodgkin-e12254`, off master 6c8f1401; no schema change) — **customers credit-note delete FK fix.** `deleteCustomer` (hard-delete path), `deleteAllCustomers`, and `deleteImportedCustomers` in `customers.service.ts` all ran `creditNote.deleteMany` while `OrderCreditNote` rows could still reference the notes being deleted. `OrderCreditNote.creditNoteId` has NO `onDelete` (Prisma default = Restrict; only the `orderId` side cascades), so for any tenant with order↔credit-note links (minted whenever a credit note is applied against an order) the delete threw an FK violation and aborted the entire `tenantTransaction` — the bulk delete-all endpoint 500'd. All three sites now fetch the doomed notes' ids and run `orderCreditNote.deleteMany({ creditNoteId: { in } })` first, mirroring the method's existing returns-before-orders child-before-parent pattern. Verified `OrderCreditNote` is the ONLY Restrict child of `CreditNote`: `InvoicePayment.creditNoteId` is optional (SetNull), `CreditNoteItem` cascades, and `Return.creditNoteId` / `RegulatedSalesLedger.creditNoteId` are plain columns with no FK relation. 4 new specs in `customers.service.spec.ts` (three `order↔credit-note links` describe blocks) assert the link delete happens and precedes the note delete via `mock.invocationCallOrder`. ⚠️ Landing order: the `fix/destructive-endpoint-guards` PR (B126/B127, plan at `.claude/pipeline/2026-08-30-destructive-endpoint-guards/`) adds a PAID/SENT pre-flight to these same methods and fixes the `settings.controller clearFinancialData` twin of this FK bug (its R8) — land this branch AFTER it and rebase.
- **2026-08-29** — (branch `fix/turbo-global-dependencies`, off master a545a10d; no schema change) — TURBO'S CACHE KEY NOW INCLUDES THE LOCKFILE AND EVERY MANIFEST. `turbo.json` had no `globalDependencies` at all, so the entire global hash was ONE file: `.gitattributes`. Turbo hashes the dependency GRAPH it resolves out of `package-lock.json`, not the lockfile itself — measured on a545a10d by mutating one file at a time and diffing all 28 task hashes from `turbo run check-types lint test --dry=json`: a transitive dep's `version` change moved 28/28, but an `integrity` change (so a DIFFERENT tarball installs), a `dev`/`devOptional` metadata flip (the drift a foreign npm regenerates on a Dependabot lock PR), and whitespace ALL moved 0/28; root `package.json` moved 0/28 (so edits to the `verify` script's own definition invalidated nothing) and a workspace manifest moved only its OWN package's 3/28, never its dependents'. That is the third false-green mechanism found 2026-08-29 and the one behind #495's `18/18 CACHED, FULL TURBO, 4.6s` on a lockfile-only change (independently reproduced on a second branch whose replayed log reported 3130 api tests "passing" that never executed). Fix adds ONLY `globalDependencies: ["package-lock.json", "package.json", "apps/*/package.json", "packages/*/package.json"]` — the `tasks` block is byte-identical to master. All six mutations now move 28/28. ⚠️ `globalCacheInputs.files` resolves to exactly 11 entries (`.gitattributes` + the lock + the 9 git-tracked manifests) with ZERO node_modules paths — turbo hashes git-tracked files and node_modules is gitignored, so the walk cannot reach the 1896+ manifests under it; a bare `**/package.json` resolves to the identical 11 files, and the explicit list is kept only because it cannot be MISREAD as walking node_modules. TRADE-OFF, accepted: any dependency change now busts EVERY task's cache, so the first local run after a dep bump is cold by design (measured cold `npm run verify` = 12m15s, warm replay = seconds). ⚠️ CI is NOT affected and needs no matching change — it has no turbo cache whatsoever (no TURBO_TOKEN/TURBO_TEAM, no actions/cache of `.turbo`, fresh runner per job), so every CI run was already cold; the ONLY cache in ci.yml is `actions/setup-node`'s `cache: npm`, which that action already keys on package-lock.json. Cold-start cost lands on local runs and the pre-push hook only.
- **2026-08-29** — (branch `fix/e2e-health-gate-and-boxed01`, off master 5603ee63) — **THE E2E DEPLOY-READINESS GATE IS NOW REAL.** ci.yml's E2E job used to "Wait for API health": it polled the **API** service while every spec hits the **web** service, and on run 33263396288 it returned 200 in ONE SECOND from the _previous_ deploy — so the suite ran against a web build that did not match the checkout (proof: the deployed app served #474's NEW copy while the checked-out spec asserted the OLD one, i.e. the app was AHEAD of the spec). The job structurally could not tell "the app is broken" from "the app is one commit behind". Now it blocks until the deployed WEB build matches `github.sha`, via a new **`apps/web/app/api/health/route.ts`** (the web app's first and only route handler) returning `{status, sha, branch, timestamp}` from `RAILWAY_GIT_COMMIT_SHA`. ⚠️ `force-dynamic` + `revalidate = 0` are LOAD-BEARING — a statically prerendered copy would freeze the build-time SHA and Railway's edge would serve it forever, wedging the gate into an infinite wait. ⚠️ **Railway injects `RAILWAY_GIT_COMMIT_SHA`/`RAILWAY_GIT_BRANCH` into the RUNNING container but they do NOT appear in `railway variables`** (that lists only service-scoped vars) — absence there is NOT absence at runtime; verified `railway ssh --service @routeflow/web 'printf "%s" "$RAILWAY_GIT_COMMIT_SHA"'` → `5603ee63…` == origin/master exactly. ⚠️ The gate deliberately does NOT require `sha == github.sha`: Railway deploys per service via watchPatterns, so an **api-only push legitimately leaves web on an older commit and never redeploys it** — exact-match would false-red every such push. An older web SHA passes ONLY when `git diff --name-only <deployed> <github.sha> -- apps/web packages` is EMPTY. That diff needs both commits present, hence **`fetch-depth: 0` on the E2E job's checkout ONLY** (the other six stay depth-1); without it the diff hits `fatal: bad object` and either kills the step (false red on every api-only push) or, if defensively `|| true`'d, silently degrades to always-pass. A failed diff instead sets `drift=__unresolved__`, KEEPS WAITING, and fails loudly ("that commit is not in this checkout" — the `railway up` / rewritten-history case). Budget 900s, job timeout 25→45 min. **KNOWN GAP, stated not hidden:** the API is probed for REACHABILITY ONLY — `app.service.ts` returns `{status,timestamp}` with no SHA, so an api-only deploy still in flight is undetected; closing it means adding the same field there, read at REQUEST time (not module load, or a cached value defeats it). ⚠️ Guard invariant when touching ci.yml: `^\s*run: npm ci --engine-strict=false` = **5** in ci.yml, 2 in nightly, 2 in post-deploy (raw string count is also 5 in ci.yml — measured at HEAD 5603ee63 and after this change, so raw and run-step counts agree and either is a usable guard). `--engine-strict=false` is load-bearing: root `.npmrc` sets `engine-strict=true` and prisma 7.10's dev-only transitive declares node>=22 against Node-20 runners, so a bare `npm ci` hard-fails EBADENGINE on every job. Middleware does not intercept `/api/health` (line 42 exempts `/api/` from the mobile-UA rewrite; the landing 307 is scoped to `pathname === "/"`; the buyer guard needs an `rf-buyer-auth` cookie a CI curl never sends). ⚠️ Context for anyone planning around this: **`post-deploy.yml` has NEVER run** (its Railway deploy hook was never wired) and **`nightly.yml` fails every night on Actions billing, not on tests** (both jobs `started_at: null`) — so the master-push E2E job is the ONLY E2E signal that exists, and the old "nightly is the authoritative green" note is false.

- **2026-08-29** — (branch `fix/e2e-and-analytics-spec-absorb`, off master 0b2c3a0a) — **ROOT-CAUSED AND FIXED THE CHRONIC MASTER E2E RED**, plus absorbed 8 orphaned analytics spec cases. (1) THE E2E FIX: `E2E (Playwright)` had been red on master for weeks (0b2c3a0a, 5cb71545, 96826793, 28cb0a25, 424c7201) while all six other jobs passed — and it was NEVER an app problem (post-deploy-check + feature-smoke were green against production throughout). `apps/web/e2e/helpers/auth.ts` `fillWorkspaceIfShown` did `isVisible()`-then-`fill()`. `isVisible()` is a POINT-IN-TIME check with no auto-wait: the server-rendered login form shows the Workspace field for a frame before the client derives the tenant from the hostname and drops it, so `isVisible()` returned true, hydration removed the node, and the following `fill()` burned the full 20s `actionTimeout` and threw `TimeoutError: locator.fill … waiting for getByLabel('Workspace')`. Because EVERY role project `depends on` the `setup` project, that ONE failure failed the whole job — signature: **operator FAILS outright while customer goes FLAKY on the identical code path** (pure timing). Replaced with a single atomic `await page.getByLabel("Workspace").fill(slug, { timeout: 5_000 })` in a try/catch: `fill()` auto-waits so a field appearing mid-hydration is still handled, and one that never appears costs only the short timeout. ⚠️ This is NOT a vacuous green — if the workspace value were genuinely required, the `waitForURL("**/dashboard")` immediately after fails loudly. MEASURED against production: before = operator FAILED + customer flaky; after = operator ok 11.6s, customer ok 10.0s, super-admin skipped (its `PLAYWRIGHT_SA_*` secrets are absent — expected). ⚠️ RULED OUT, do not re-chase: `global.setup.ts` is well-behaved — with no `E2E_SEED_DATABASE_URL` in CI it logs and `return`s cleanly rather than throwing; the absent seed secret is BY DESIGN (CI targets the deployed app's standing `e2e-routeflow` tenant). ⚠️ **You can reproduce E2E failures WITHOUT a public window** — Actions logs are unreadable while the repo is private (`gh run view --log` ⇒ "log not found", and the `if: failure()` playwright-report artifact never uploads; only `coverage-reports` comes back). Instead: `cd apps/web && CI=true SKIP_E2E_SEED=true PLAYWRIGHT_BASE_URL=https://routeflowweb-production.up.railway.app PLAYWRIGHT_TENANT_SLUG=e2e-routeflow npx playwright test --project=setup`. `test-results/<test>/error-context.md` carries the full a11y page snapshot — that is what proved the Workspace field genuinely does not exist on that host. Start with `--project=setup`: everything depends on it. (2) THE SPEC ABSORPTION: the abandoned `focused-kalam` worktree held 8 test cases that existed NOWHERE in master. A branch audit had called it "superseded" because master's implementation is a strict SUPERSET (it adds on-time-rate/stops-per-hour/avg-duration) and master had MORE cases overall (43 vs 39) — but the case SETS were not nested. Ported onto master's real implementation (mocks re-faithful to the superset shape): controller 5→7 cases, service 44→50. `analytics.controller.spec.ts` gained a "route/driver performance" block pinning that `getRoutes`/`getDrivers` forward from/to to BOTH service methods and forward absent params as `undefined` — **the shipped from/to wiring had ZERO controller coverage until now**, so its mock now stubs `getRoutePerformance`/`getDriverPerformance` alongside `getProductDemand`. ⚠️ LESSON: when judging whether a branch is superseded, diff the TEST-CASE TITLE SETS, not just the implementation — a higher total case count can still hide missing coverage.

- **2026-08-29** — (branch `chore/drop-jest-native`, stacked on `fix/react-test-renderer-pin` #494 -> `chore/normalize-lock-npm10` #495) — DROPPED the deprecated `@testing-library/jest-native@5.4.3` from apps/mobile: one manifest line, -117 lock lines, ZERO test-code changes. npm prints the deprecation on every install ("use the built-in Jest matchers available in @testing-library/react-native v12.4+"), and mobile already carries react-native@13.3.3. ⚠️ THERE WAS NOTHING TO MIGRATE — jest-native was never wired up: `apps/mobile/jest.config.js` has NO `setupFiles`/`setupFilesAfterEach`/`setupFilesAfterEnv` key at all, no jest setup file exists on disk, and NEITHER @testing-library package is imported anywhere under `__tests__/`. Mobile's suite is `preset: ts-jest` + `testEnvironment: "node"` + `testMatch: **/__tests__/**/*.test.ts` — pure-logic only, no component tests, exactly as CLAUDE.md states. So `extend-expect` was never loaded and no jest-native matcher was ever callable; this is a pure manifest deletion, not a migration. The 8 lock entries removed are jest-native's OWN nested jest-29 island (@jest/schemas, jest-matcher-utils, pretty-format x2, ansi-styles, jest-diff, react-is), NOT any hoist the edge validator's TOLERATED_SKEWS baseline matches on — hence no baseline edit: 2637 -> 2629 entries with missing 0 / skew 0 new / 8 tolerated / override-forced 23 / peer 4 unchanged. ⚠️ THIS DOES NOT FREE THE `react-test-renderer` PIN AND THE PIN MUST NOT BE REMOVED AS "NOW REDUNDANT": `@testing-library/react-native@13.3.3` carries its own NON-optional `react-test-renderer >=18.2.0` peer (its `peerDependenciesMeta` marks only `jest` optional), so the float survives jest-native's removal. Measured across four from-scratch resolutions: both TL packages + no pin -> ERESOLVE (via jest-native >=16.0.0); jest-native removed + no pin -> STILL ERESOLVE (via react-native >=18.2.0); both TL packages removed + no pin -> resolves; pin present -> resolves. Freeing the pin would mean deleting the sanctioned component-testing library to save one manifest line — bad trade. Gates: `npm run validate-lock` exit 0 (numbers above); `npm@10.8.2 ci --dry-run` exit 0 / zero Invalid-Missing, reporting "added 80, removed 8"; from-scratch manifests-only resolution completes (2419 entries); mobile jest 90/90 files, 1242 tests run DIRECTLY. ⚠️ Do NOT gate lock changes on `npm run verify` alone — it returned 18/18 FULL TURBO CACHED in 4.6s on a lock-only change (turbo's key omits package-lock.json); and on a saturated box a forced run produced a FALSE RED (a 5000ms timeout in import-customer-cap.spec.ts that passes 4/4 in isolation). CI on clean runners is the authoritative gate.

- **2026-08-29 — CONSOLIDATION COMPLETE (re-anchor `mappedSha` → 5cb71545).** Fifteen PRs landed across five public windows, every one CI-gated, deployed, and verified by `post-deploy-check` + `feature-smoke`: #487 #476 #474 #478 #481 #477 #486 #488 · #490 #485 #492 · #495 #494 #493 #475. **Two permanent gates now live on master:** the lock-edge validator (`npm run validate-lock` — step 1 of `verify`, so the pre-push hook gates the lock, plus a PRE-install `Lockfile integrity` CI job, because `npm ci` itself dies on an override-break and a post-install gate could never run on the case it exists to explain), and the bug-signature scanner (`npm run scan` + its own CI job, baselined in `scan-ignore.json` so only NEW instances fail). **Gate-trust rules learned the hard way — see `project_merge_train_2026-08-28` memory:** three distinct FALSE-GREEN mechanisms (turbo replaying pass-through-arg runs; a session re-running a cached copy of a moving script; and **turbo not hashing `package-lock.json`**, so a lock-only change replays everything in 4.6s — dependency PRs were effectively unverified on a warm cache, fix chipped) and one FALSE-RED (host saturation reaping jest workers → bare `exited (1)` with NO jest report; a real failure always prints `Tests: N failed` first). Rule: read the `Cached: N` line AND confirm jest reported, before believing either colour; on a saturated box CI on clean runners beats a local forced run. **Repo cleanup:** worktrees 16→9, remote branches 12→2, local branches 31→12, ~9-12 GB reclaimed. Owner decisions recorded: mobile driver app STAYS dev-gated (B33 = by design, not a defect); scanner ships wired into `verify`; Node 20→22 deferred as ONE deliberate move.
- **2026-08-29** — (branches `chore/normalize-lock-npm10` #495 -> `fix/react-test-renderer-pin` #494, stacked, off master 2af5e9ef) — PINNED `react-test-renderer`, UNBLOCKING EVERY FUTURE FULL LOCK REGENERATION, plus a lock normalization the rebase uncovered. (1) THE PIN: a from-scratch resolution had stopped completing — `react-test-renderer` was declared and pinned NOWHERE, entering only as a FLOATING PEER of the two @testing-library packages (`jest-native@5.4.3` `>=16.0.0`, `react-native@13.3.3` `>=18.2.0`), so npm 10.8.2 resolving the manifests alone picked the registry's 19.2.8, whose `react@^19.2.8` peer collides with mobile's exact `react@19.2.0` → ERESOLVE. Nothing broke day-to-day because `npm ci` installs the pinned lock — the lock was holding the conflict back, exactly the registry-drift exposure #488 bought. Fixed with `"react-test-renderer": "19.2.0"` EXACT in apps/mobile devDependencies. ⚠️ WORKSPACE devDependency, NOT root `overrides`, FOR A STRUCTURAL REASON: this lock records NO `overrides` field at all, so an overrides pin leaves ZERO trace in the lock and `npm ci`'s own validator can never check it, whereas a workspace devDependency lands in `packages["apps/mobile"].devDependencies` and is checked every install. Both candidates resolved from scratch to BYTE-IDENTICAL trees apart from that one manifest line, so the choice was free. Bump the pin WITH mobile's `react` and jest-expo's pin (verified jest-expo@55.0.22 still declares react-test-renderer 19.2.0 exactly) — same discipline as `expo-modules-core`. NEVER `--legacy-peer-deps`. ⚠️ MEASURED: retiring `@testing-library/jest-native` does NOT free the pin — react-native@13.3.3 carries its own NON-optional `react-test-renderer >=18.2.0` peer (peerDependenciesMeta marks only `jest` optional); from-scratch across four cases: both TL packages+no pin → ERESOLVE, jest-native removed+no pin → STILL ERESOLVE, both TL removed+no pin → resolves, pin present → resolves. THE PIN STAYS. (2) THE NORMALIZATION (#495, lock-only, landed first): rebasing revealed the documented regen recipe is no longer a no-op on master — #485 (dependabot) regenerated the lock with an npm outside our 10.8.2 rule, introducing `libc` (4 lightningcss musl/glibc entries) and inflating `devOptional` 139→296 (libc=0/devOptional=139 held from 87ae7ea5 through ecfd8efb; changed at 424c7201). Control test: master's manifests+lock UNCHANGED under `npx -y npm@10.8.2 install --package-lock-only` rewrites 354 lines. Local npm 11.9.0 doesn't reproduce master's shape either (strips libc, keeps devOptional 296) — a THIRD npm wrote it. Normalization proven package-neutral entry-by-entry: 2637→2637 entries, key sets identical, version diffs 0, integrity diffs 0, resolved diffs 0; only devOptional(157)/dev(185)/libc(4) fields differ. Split into its own PR so a repo-wide lock rewrite isn't buried in a one-line pin; #494 then collapses to its genuine 1 lock line. ⚠️ `npm run verify` was 18/18 FULL TURBO CACHED on the lock change — meaningless as a gate; real greens came from direct `npx jest`: api 180 suites/3130 tests, mobile 90/1242. Open follow-up (chip task_a93e4f99): npm 10 records no `libc`, prod is node:20-alpine (musl) — unproven whether npm 10 can mis-select a glibc lightningcss binary; pre-existing, both lock shapes have deployed SUCCESS. Local gotcha: `npm ci --dry-run` and `--package-lock-only` BOTH exit 1 on the husky `prepare` hook when node_modules is absent — the lock work already succeeded; re-check with `--ignore-scripts`.

- **2026-08-29** — LOCKFILE EDGE VALIDATOR AS A CI GATE (worktree branch `claude/ecstatic-pascal-eb38e6`; tooling + docs only, no app code): recovered the ad-hoc validator written during #488's second CI round and landed it as `scripts/validate-lock-edges.mjs` + `npm run validate-lock`, wired into ci.yml's **Lint** job right after install (~1s, fails fast before the slower jobs spend minutes). It does what arborist never does — walks EVERY lock entry's declared deps by node_modules walk-up with root `overrides` applied — and gates on three findings: **MISSING** (the #488 class: six jest coverage-chain packages absent from the lock entirely, lazily required only under `jest --coverage`, a CI-only flag), **workspace override break** (a workspace range the root override pin falls outside — this is #488's ROOT CAUSE, npm 10's `ci` validator being override-unaware where npm 11 is not, producing the ~97-error phantom-subtree cascade that names everything except the real problem), and **SKEW** outside the `TOLERATED_SKEWS` baseline. Both historical failures were replayed against scratch copies of the lock and both exit 1. Hardened over the ad-hoc original: overrides now read from `package.json` (the lock root carries NO `overrides` field — the original's cwd fallback was load-bearing and easy to drop, and without it 29 override-forced edges masquerade as skews); npm-alias edges resolve by the ALIAS name (the original looked up the target and would have reported a phantom MISSING); and `semver.validRange` guards every comparison because `semver.satisfies` SWALLOWS a parse error and returns false — `tailwindcss-animate`'s published `">=3.0.0 || insiders"` peer was a false mismatch. Baseline: **0 missing, 0 workspace breaks, 8 tolerated skews, 23 override-forced, 4 unsatisfied peers.** All 8 skews are `dev:true` jest-toolchain only, triaged in `docs/testing/lockfile-edges.md` as three internally-consistent coexisting jest generations (30.2.0 = what the suites run on, with `jest`/`jest-cli` nesting their own copies; a 30.5.x hoist island nothing on the run path reaches; a 29.7.0 island from jest-expo) — **none fix-now**, since 7 need a from-scratch regeneration and the 8th would mean a 9th jest `overrides` entry for no behavioural gain. ⚠️ NEW FINDING while verifying the retire path: **a from-scratch resolution no longer completes** — `react-test-renderer` is declared and pinned NOWHERE (floating peer of `@testing-library/jest-native@5.4.3`'s `>=16.0.0`), the lock holds 19.2.0 matching mobile's exact `react@19.2.0`, and the registry has moved to 19.2.8 which demands `react@^19.2.8` → ERESOLVE. Nothing is broken today (the pinned lock is holding the conflict back — precisely #488's value), but pin it before any full regen; chipped as task_916d5117. ⚠️ TWO VERIFIED CORRECTIONS to the react-test-renderer write-up (from exciting-feynman, both re-checked here against the lock): (1) pinning it in a WORKSPACE devDependency vs root `overrides` are NOT interchangeable — this lock records NO `overrides` field (the same fact that forces the validator to read them from package.json), so an overrides pin leaves ZERO trace in the lock and npm ci's own validator can never check it, while a workspace devDep lands in packages["apps/mobile"].devDependencies and is checked every install (from-scratch trees are byte-identical apart from the one manifest line, so the verifiable option is free); (2) retiring the deprecated `@testing-library/jest-native` does NOT make the pin redundant — `@testing-library/react-native@13.3.3` declares its own NON-optional `react-test-renderer >=18.2.0` peer (peerDependenciesMeta marks only `jest` optional, confirmed straight from the lock), so the float survives; only removing BOTH testing-library packages frees it. Do not drop the pin as dead weight. Also documented: the react 19-vs-18 peer lines are load-bearing evidence for web's deliberate `--force --no-save react@18.3.1` Docker root-swap. **CONSOLIDATION (same day): #489 (hungry-colden, `scripts/lock-validate.mjs`) built the same guard in parallel; routeflow-d8 broke the tie for #490 on the override-incoherence fixture (#489 exits 0 on it) and on semver fidelity, and #490 absorbed #489's two placement wins.** Final shape after three measured corrections: (1) the validator is now `npm run verify` **step 1**, so the pre-push hook — the authoritative gate while CI billing is flaky — gates the lock too; (2) CI runs it as a **`lock-integrity` job PRE-install with the FULL gate**, and the Lint-job step is GONE — because `npm ci` ITSELF dies on an override-break (verified: mobile jest ^30.3.0 vs the 30.2.0 pin ⇒ `Missing: jest@30.5.0 / @jest/core@30.5.0 / camelcase@6.3.0`), so no post-install step could ever gate the case the check exists to explain; (3) ⚠️ the prescribed `npm i --no-save semver@7` before it is UNUSABLE — `npm install <pkg>` at a workspaces ROOT re-resolves the entire workspace tree from the registry (measured >10 min, never finished — the very drift #488 removed); semver goes into an **isolated empty scratch dir + `NODE_PATH`** (the `apps/mobile/Dockerfile` mechanism), pinned to the version the tree hoists so CI and pre-push cannot reach different verdicts, **asserted on load** (`EXPECTED_SEMVER_MAJOR`, exit 2 on mismatch — observability alone would let comparison semantics drift silently, the same class this PR closes) and printed in the summary. New `--edges-only` mode = zero-dep reachability; it is an EXPLICIT flag, never inferred, so a missing semver is exit 2 rather than a green run that skipped most of its gates. Also fixed a latent false positive from hungry-colden's review: a name in BOTH `dependencies` and `optionalDependencies` is OPTIONAL (npm documents the optional entry overrides; arborist's later edge replaces the earlier), so its absence is a VALID tree — the walk had the inversion (0 occurrences in today's lock).
- **2026-08-28** — NOTIFICATION BELL TENANT-SCOPED STORAGE (worktree branch `claude/xenodochial-volhard-be1098`; web-only, one file): `lib/hooks/useNotifications.ts` persists the bell feed under `rf_notifications:<slug>` instead of the global `rf_notifications` — the global key leaked the previous tenant's history to a super-admin impersonating another tenant (or any same-browser tenant switch). Slug comes from `useTenant()` (TenantProvider's `resolveTenantSlug`: JWT wins, cookie self-heals, re-resolves on focus/visibility); the hook re-hydrates on slug resolve/switch and writes through a `slugRef` so `push`/`markAllRead`/`clear` keep stable identities for the socket effect. No slug ⇒ no read/write. Legacy global key is deleted on load, never migrated (its rows can't be attributed to a tenant — migrating IS the leak).

Existing backlog baselined in `.claude/skills/bug-hunt/scan-ignore.json` (165 entries, file-level or file+matched-text) so it fails ONLY on newly introduced instances — ⚠️ this baseline is load-bearing for the gate: drop it or regenerate it from a partial scan and CI either floods with false hits or goes blind to real ones; `// scan-ok: <id> — reason` suppresses inline. Wired as standalone `bug-scan` CI job (`.github/workflows/ci.yml`, ~2s, no install needed) AND as fail-fast step 1 of `npm run verify` (`"verify": "npm run scan && turbo run check-types lint test"`); new `npm run scan` / `npm run scan:all` (--verbose) scripts. Companion forensic pair also landed: `scripts/data-integrity-report.mjs` (read-only, 25 SELECT-only prod-safety checks, `default_transaction_read_only` session) + `scripts/repair-integrity.mjs` (surgical, hardcoded-allowlist repair for the 23 specific rows that report found 2026-08-28/29; refuses outside `PROD_DB_NAMES = {railway, routeflow}`) + `scripts/REPAIR-RUNBOOK.md` — see api.md for detail. - **2026-08-28** — B40 ANALYTICS DATE-RANGE, remaining three cards (worktree branch `claude/charming-jennings-91af63`; no schema change; complements PR #478's route/driver-performance half): `getDso(from?, to?)` windows PAID invoices on `Invoice.issueDate` via new private `issueDateFilter` (same shape as #478's `runDateFilter`: no bounds ⇒ no filter, mobile's parameterless calls stay all-time; the range selects which invoices count — payment may land after `to`). `getDeadStock(daysInactive, from?, to?)`: a picker range REPLACES the rolling `daysInactive` window (dead ⇔ no stock movement AND no invoiced sale inside `[from,to]`; movement query gains the matching lte bound; parameterless rolling behavior byte-identical via open-ended gte; `lastMovement`/`daysInactive` display stays most-recent-EVER, and stock is always current-state — a past range is "which of today's stocked products didn't move then", not a snapshot). `getMarginAlerts` DECIDED range-independent (today's price vs today's averageCost — no history exists): server untouched, web stops sending/refetching from-to and both inventory cards gained captions stating their window semantics; dead-stock web row now renders `daysInactive: null` as an em dash (was "nulld"). Controller: dead-stock + dso gained `@Query from/to`; margin-alerts deliberately not. Specs: getDso describe (unwindowed default asserts NO issueDate filter / windowed asserts gte+23:59:59 lte / empty-window zeros) + getDeadStock range-replaces-rolling + open-ended-gte tests. - **2026-08-28** — ROUTE/DRIVER PERFORMANCE OPS METRICS (worktree branch `claude/awesome-bun-72b35d`, backlog item 6 of the competitor-matrix audit; no schema change): `analytics.service.ts` `getRoutePerformance`/`getDriverPerformance` now accept `(from?, to?)` windowing on `RouteRun.scheduledDate` via new private `runDateFilter` (no params ⇒ no filter — mobile's parameterless calls keep all-time behavior; the web Operations tab always sent from/to and the API silently ignored them until now, i.e. this also fixes the Operations half of the chipped "analytics date-filter inert" bug) and both return three new nullable metrics via shared `accumulateRunMetrics`/`finalizeRunMetrics`: `onTimeRate` (schema-verified there is NO promised-ETA field on RouteRunStop, so on-time = stop `completedAt` ≤ end of the run's `scheduledDate` UTC calendar day; denominator = completed stops only — never-completed stops are incomplete, not late), `stopsPerHour` and `avgRunDurationMinutes` (only runs with a positive startedAt→completedAt span enter duration math, excluded from BOTH numerator and denominator so unstamped runs can't poison averages; null — never NaN/Infinity/0 — when a window has no measurable data). Controller passes the two @Query params. Web `analytics/page.tsx` OperationsTab: +3 columns on both tables via shared `opsMetricColumns<T extends RunOpsMetrics>()`, null → "—", `fmtDuration` "1h 23m". Mobile `lib/api/admin.ts`: both hooks accept `(from?, to?)`, row types extend `RunOpsMetrics` (no mobile screen renders them yet). Specs: `analytics.service.spec.ts` new describe "route & driver performance" (5 tests — aggregation, invalid-run exclusion, null guards incl. zero-length span, where-clause windowing, driver variant); suite 42/42, controller spec 12/12, api tsc clean. NOTE: open PR #474 touches the same page.tsx but only the RevenueTab (pie→bars) — no functional overlap, trivial adjacency.
- **2026-08-28** — DURABLE POD ARTIFACTS (worktree branch `claude/adoring-taussig-5fdab3`; api+mobile+web, NO schema change — the existing `RouteRunStop.podPhotoUrls`/`signatureUrl` columns now hold storage keys): closes the competitor-audit finding that POD photos were persisted as device-local `file://` URIs and native signatures as the literal `"native-captured"` sentinel — the regulated gate passed but nothing was ever retrievable, and no web screen read either column. Design was forced by two constraints: the api's 2MB JSON body cap and the mobile offline queue excluding FormData (and persisting the whole queue in ONE AsyncStorage key, so multi-MB queued bodies would brick Android rehydrate). Hence: **photos** upload one-per-request as JSON data URLs (`POST /route-runs/:id/stops/:stopId/pod-artifact`, ≤1280px JPEG q0.6 ≈ 200-450KB, idempotent per client `artifactId` [djb2], fired by `payment.tsx closeStop()` BEFORE the completion so offline both queue and replay FIFO in the right order; completion no longer sends `podPhotoUrls` at all — it would overwrite keys appended by queued attaches); the **signature** is a stroke-built SVG data URL (pure JS `apps/mobile/lib/pod-artifacts.ts`, same path web+native, canvas/sentinel deleted) sent INLINE in the completion because the regulated gate demands it on that request (few KB, offline-safe). Server (`routes/pod-artifacts.util.ts` + `ingestPodCapture`/`attachPodArtifact`/`getStopPod` in routes.service): sharp-compresses (`compressImage`; SVG→raster support pinned by a new real-sharp spec case) and stores under `tenants/<tenantId>/pod/<stopId>/` (existing uploads tenant-gate + HMAC signed URLs apply), persists KEYS; legacy strings pass through unchanged (old driver builds keep completing); `GET .../pod` presigns OWN-TENANT pod keys only and reports `legacyPhotoCount`/`signatureCaptured` so the UI never lies about undisplayable legacy captures. Web `routes/[id]` expanded COMPLETED stops render `StopPodSection` (lazy `useStopPod`): photo thumbnails → full-size, signature image, age/ID verified chips, honest legacy fallbacks. Specs: routes.service POD block (9), `pod-artifacts.util.spec.ts`, mobile `__tests__/pod-artifacts.test.ts` (6), compress.util SVG case. Playwright deliberately skipped (completion mutates; e2e suite is read-only-or-cancel by design).
- **2026-08-28 (night)** — NPM-CI MIGRATION (worktree amazing-gagarin, no schema change; resolves F12-004): every install is now lockfile-exact `npm ci` — ci.yml ×5, nightly.yml ×2, post-deploy.yml ×2, web+mobile Dockerfiles (api already was). The old `rm package-lock && npm install --force` recipe re-resolved the registry every run (= how the dist-less standardwebhooks@1.1.0 broke CI despite a pinned lock). ROOT CAUSE of "npm ci fails at full checkout": apps/mobile `jest ^30.3.0` vs root override pin `jest 30.2.0` — an override OUTSIDE the declared range; npm 10.8's `ci` validator is override-unaware (npm 11 is not, hence "works locally"), floats a phantom jest@30.5.0 subtree and reports ~97 cascade Missing/Invalid errors (@parcel/watcher, glob@13, picomatch@4 = phantom's deps, not real problems). Fixed by relaxing mobile to `^30.2.0` (jest stays on the proven 30.2.0 generation from #74) + lock regen with npm@10.8.2 (`--package-lock-only`; diff = dead nested dupes removed, ZERO version floats). THREE WORKTREE-DRIFT LANDMINES exposed once installs got hermetic: (1) tsc's default typeRoots walk EVERY ancestor node_modules/@types — from a worktree that reaches the MAIN checkout's install; mobile's typecheck had been silently completing against main-checkout @types state and broke when a concurrent session consolidated it → apps/mobile/tsconfig.json now pins `typeRoots` to its own tree + workspace root. (2) packages/ui devDep `@types/react-native ^0.73.0` is the DEPRECATED EMPTY STUB (no index.d.ts — RN ships its own types); it breaks any typeRoots enumeration that can't fall through to an ancestor's ancient real copy — REMOVED. (3) the lock nested the ONLY `expo-modules-core` under node_modules/expo/node_modules — unreachable by walk-up from root expo-* packages that import it without declaring it (expo-notifications et al); under skipLibCheck the failed d.ts import silently degrades `PermissionResponse` → TS2339 `.status` in lib/auth.ts. It ever passed only via TWO crutches: the main checkout's old root-hoisted copy (worktree walk-up), and CI's fresh --force installs re-hoisting one every run. → apps/mobile now declares `expo-modules-core: 55.0.25` (exact, tracking expo's own pin — bump BOTH together), npm dedupes to a single ROOT copy. Same class as #482's expo-router NODE_PATH fix — npm nesting vs implicit expo imports; suspect this class FIRST when a fresh-lock mobile typecheck breaks on expo types. Web Dockerfile's `npm install --force --no-save react@18.3.1 react-dom@18.3.1` root-swap stays deliberately (npm ci can't add packages; exact-pinned immutable versions). Mobile Dockerfile keeps ENV NODE_PATH (#482). Verified: npm@10.8.2 `ci --dry-run` 0 errors at full checkout + staged web/mobile Docker-context replicas; real `npm ci` + full verify green; all three images built locally in Docker (npm ci layers live, web's react swap + expo export + nest build all clean). Rebased over #484 mid-flight (took master's lock, re-ran the regen on top). ⚠️ Ops: future manifest edits without a coherent lock regen now FAIL CI loudly — that is the point; fix the lock, don't resurrect --force.
- **2026-08-28** — IMPERSONATION IDENTITY FOLLOWS THE TENANT + ADMINS STOP RESURRECTING AS DRIVERS (branch `feat/dispatch-addon-enforcement`, SECOND commit — stacked on the devmode/dispatch batch below; plan `.claude/pipeline/plans/2026-08-28-impersonation-driver-fixes.md`; no schema change, no new deps). Two owner-reported bugs. **(A) Impersonation branding/identity leak.** Banner and DATA were right, but the sidebar logo/business name and the top-right username chip kept showing the PREVIOUS tenant: `AuthProvider` and `TenantProvider` snapshot identity ONCE at mount and the only subscriber to `setImpersonation`'s `rf-impersonation-change` event was the banner, while the tenants-LIST Impersonate button used `router.push` (soft nav, no remount) and never wrote the tenant-slug cookie — unlike the [id] page, which already did `setTenantCookie` + hard load. Fixed on four fronts: `lib/impersonation.ts` gained a third key `impersonationUsername` (`ImpersonationState.username`, `setImpersonation(token, slug, username?)`, cleared by `clearImpersonation`) and its `parseExp` now base64url-decodes (`-`→`+`, `_`→`/`) — `atob` on a raw segment THREW, so `expired` was pinned false forever and an expired impersonation never redirected; `subscribeImpersonation`'s `storage` listener became **key-filtered** (token/slug/username, plus `key === null` for `localStorage.clear()`) because the unfiltered one fired on EVERY cross-tab write (sidebar state, view mode…) and would now log idle tabs out through the new subscribers. `lib/auth-context.tsx` + `components/tenant-provider.tsx` both subscribe (`setUser(getStoredUser())` / the existing slug-changed `recheck`) — each applies only a NON-NULL read, and AuthProvider deliberately does NOT fall back to `refreshTokens()` (a null read mid-transition must not re-pin the old session). `admin/tenants/page.tsx` now matches the [id] page exactly (`setImpersonation(..., res.data.impersonatedUser?.username)` + `setTenantCookie` + `window.location.href = "/dashboard"`), and the banner's Exit is a hard load onto `/admin/tenants` instead of `router.push` so every provider remounts on the operator session (no re-pin helper exists — the super-admin's own session carries no tenant slug). Banner copy is now `acting as {imp.username ?? "Tenant Admin"}`. **(B) A deleted admin kept reappearing in Drivers.** Platform-admin `createTenant` AND `createTenantAdmin` auto-created a Driver row for the new admin and hardcoded `canActAsDriver: true`; `DELETE /drivers/:id` then hard-deleted the Driver AND the linked User in one tx — on an admin with restrict-FK children (messages, stock counts, device tokens) the user-delete failed, the WHOLE tx rolled back, and the driver row "came back"; had it succeeded it would have deleted the tenant's ADMIN LOGIN. Owner decision: **admins are NOT drivers by default** — both `driver.create` blocks are gone and both flags write `false`, leaving Settings → "Act as driver" (`users.service.toggleDriverPermit`, INTENTIONALLY unchanged) as the sole explicit opt-in that creates the row. `drivers.service.remove()` now reads the linked `user.role` inside the tx and branches: pure `DRIVER` logins are still deleted with the profile, dual-role OPERATOR/TENANT_ADMIN logins are NEVER deleted and get `canActAsDriver: false` in the same tx — clearing the flag is what makes the deletion stick (otherwise the toggle silently resurrects it). New `describe("remove")` in `drivers.service.spec.ts` covers both branches + the preserved scheduled/in-progress run guard. **Fixed in passing:** `auth/strategies/jwt.strategy.ts` `validate()` never copied `canActAsDriver` into `req.user`, so `RolesGuard`'s dual-role branch (operators/tenant-admins with the capability satisfying `@Roles(DRIVER)`) was DEAD server-side — it now defaults false through; this only WIDENS access for intended dual-role users. The flag is minted at login, so a driver-permit change still needs re-login (matches the existing Settings toast copy). The plan also proposed propagating `isAdmin` alongside it; review caught that it is NOT widen-only — `req.user.isAdmin` feeds exactly one consumer, the `PATCH /users/:id/admin` gate (`users.controller.ts`: `user.isAdmin || role === TENANT_ADMIN`), which passes only TENANT_ADMINs today precisely BECAUSE the claim is dropped. Since the token mints `isAdmin: user.isAdmin || role === "TENANT_ADMIN"`, propagating it would have let any admin-flagged OPERATOR grant/revoke admin rights on other operators — an unreviewed privilege-delegation path (invisible in the UI, whose gate is role-only). So `isAdmin` is deliberately left out with a comment saying why; adding it needs an owner decision plus a mirrored web gate. Explicitly out of scope: the tenant-global `rf_notifications` localStorage key (notification bleed across tenants), `refreshTokens()` cookie re-pinning (unreachable now that entry/exit are hard loads), `queryClient.clear()`, mobile, and any data-cleanup script — existing ghost driver rows are deleted once via the UI after deploy and now stay gone.
- **2026-08-28** — DEVELOPER_MODE NARROWED + DISPATCH API SERVER-ENFORCED (branch `feat/dispatch-addon-enforcement`, plan `.claude/pipeline/plans/2026-08-28-devmode-narrowing.md`; no schema change, no new deps). Two owner decisions landed together. **(1) `developer_mode` stops unlocking the two GA delivery features.** It was a master switch OR-ed into every client gate (`devMode || feature`), which made the platform-admin toggles for `recurring_routes`/`order_delivery` a no-op on any dev-mode tenant. Web + mobile `lib/api/addons.ts` `useDeliveryAccess()`/`useRoutesAccess()` now return the feature hook UNCHANGED (return shape identical, so no call site moved); every `devMode ||` disjunct is gone from `(dashboard)/layout.tsx` (nav, `RouteGuard`, Header drive-mode pill + avatar menu, `g r`/`g d` chords + SHORTCUTS), `dashboard/page.tsx`, `dispatch/page.tsx`, `CommandPalette.tsx`. `useDeveloperMode` stays exported but has **zero remaining callers in `apps/web`**; on mobile it keeps exactly the in-development surfaces — `app/_layout.tsx` driver branch, `(auth)/role-picker.tsx`, `(tenant)/_layout.tsx` dispatch tab, and the RAW drive-mode reads in `(tabs)/{home,more}.tsx` (all byte-identical by design). **(2) The dispatch API is now addon-enforced (403), mirroring the `driver_payments` precedent** — it was JWT+role only. `@RequireAddon` became variadic/any-of (`SetMetadata(REQUIRE_ADDON_KEY, addonKeys)`); `AddonGuard` accepts BOTH the legacy single string and the new array and resolves any-of with ONE `getActiveAddons(tenantId)` round-trip. Class-level gates: `RoutesController`, `RouteRunsController`, `DriversController`, `RouteOptimizationController`, `RouteTemplateOptimizationController` → `("recurring_routes","order_delivery","developer_mode")` (SHARED — an ad-hoc delivery materializes a route template + run, so a delivery-only tenant must keep runs/detail/optimize/complete); `TripsController` → `("order_delivery","developer_mode")`, with `PATCH /trips/routes/:routeId/planning` re-declaring the either-gate at handler level (it is saved from the shared run-detail page; handler metadata wins in `getAllAndOverride`). `trips/drivers/route-optimization` modules gained `BillingModule`. **Two traps worth remembering:** (a) the 403 message is toasted verbatim by web, so `INTERNAL_ADDON_KEYS = {"developer_mode"}` is honoured as an any-of key but filtered out of the text (single-key wording when one purchasable key remains, "not enabled for your account" when none) — spec'd; (b) turning ungated endpoints into 403s means **every client query must carry `enabled:` on the same gate that hides what it feeds**, or an unentitled tenant polls 403s — hence `enabled` passthroughs added to web `useRoutes`/`useRouteRuns`/`useDrivers`, the dashboard's two 30s polls, mobile `(tabs)/home.tsx`'s four queries, and `customers/[id]`'s `AssignRouteModal` (always MOUNTED — `isOpen` is a prop, not a mount condition — so it fired a 403 `/routes` fetch on every customer page). Seeds: `demo-seed.js` switched the three FEATURE addons (`driver_payments`, `recurring_routes`, `order_delivery`) to `update: {}` so a reseed creates the row when missing but PRESERVES a platform-admin toggle, while `developer_mode` stays forced active (the demo's mobile driver walkthrough needs it, and it no longer leaks into web); `e2e-seed.js` generalized `ensureDeveloperMode` → `ensureAddon(tenantId, addonKey)` and grants all three on BOTH paths (the e2e canary stays `update: { active: true }`) — the web suite exercises `/routes` and `/deliveries`, which now need the real feature addons. New spec `apps/api/src/routes/dispatch-addon-gate.spec.ts` pins each class's key array, AddonGuard-after-JwtAuthGuard ordering, and the `updatePlanning` override; `addon.guard.spec.ts` extended for any-of, legacy-string metadata and internal-key redaction; e2e `02-operator` OP-03b renamed to the dispatch-addon canary and `20-trip-builder-gate` follows `order_delivery` alone. `packages/types/index.ts` + the platform-admin `AVAILABLE_ADDONS` Developer-Mode copy are doc/copy only — the exported constant VALUES are unchanged.

Now every Anthropic call site records a tenant-tagged event (`prisma.getTenantId()`): vendor-bill scan `ocr.vendor_bill` (haiku-4-5), supplier-statement scan `ocr.supplier_statement` (sonnet-5), expense-receipt extract `ocr.expense_receipt` (opus-4-5, lifted into `RECEIPT_MODEL` const), route insights `insights.route` (resolveModel). Key resolution UNIFIED: the three OCR paths' hand-rolled `storedKey ‖ env` replaced with `platformConfig.resolveAnthropicKey(tenantKey)` (tenant SystemConfig → platform PlatformConfig → env — platform-stored keys now work for OCR, previously route-analysis only); their unused `ConfigService` injections dropped; `PlatformAdminModule` imported by vendor-bills/supplier-statements/bookkeeping modules (acyclic — it pulls only Email+Billing). `getAiUsage` classifies `ocr.*`/`forecast*`/`insights.*` and returns NEW `insightRuns`; admin settings page renders a "Route insights" row. Specs: metering asserts in vendor-bills/supplier-statements/bookkeeping specs + NEW `route-analysis.service.spec.ts` (4 cases incl. the parse-failure-books-spend-once guard); 7 other spec files' provider lists swapped ConfigService→PlatformConfigService mock. ⚠️ FLAGGED TO OWNER, not implemented: `addon.ocr` (plan-catalog.constants.ts) exists but NO scan endpoint enforces it — decide whether OCR should be entitlement-gated (`OCR_PACK_250` SKU also exists). - **2026-08-28** — DEPENDABOT #463 REMEDIATION (worktree loving-pare, branch `dependabot/npm_and_yarn/minor-and-patch-553e3723d7`; no schema change; NO source-code edits — versions only): the 99-bump minor-and-patch group failed CI in two areas, both root-caused to inappropriate bumps rather than our code/specs. (1) API — 9 suites (orders, customers, buyer, supplier-statements, vendor-bills, strip-html.transform…) died AT IMPORT: sanitize-html 2.17.5→2.17.7 swapped in an ESM-only htmlparser2 12 that Jest's CJS loader cannot require ("Cannot use import statement outside a module"), and ≥2.17.6 requires Node ≥22.12 vs our node:20-alpine prod containers; the sanitization behavior itself was verified to still round-trip every strip-html spec case on 2.17.7, so this was NOT a spec problem — pinned `sanitize-html` to exact 2.17.5 in apps/api (skipped 2.17.6/2.17.7 CVEs need allowed svg/math/animation tags; moot under our `allowedTags: []`). Zero real test failures: 2557/2557 pass. Lockfile fully regenerated from the pinned manifests (incremental `npm install` had left a broken duplicate tree: RN 0.87 hoisted at root + 0.83.10 nested under apps/mobile). - **2026-08-26** — WP1 CLIENT-RELEASE-BLOCKERS: order edits now settle stock (branch `fix/client-release-blockers`; plan `.claude/pipeline/plans/2026-08-25-client-release-blockers.md`; no schema change): `orders.service.ts` `assertStockAvailableForEdit` (validate-only) renamed+refactored into `settleStockForEdit` (validate THEN write) — held/requested compared over the UNION of product ids (not just requested's keys) so a removed/zeroed line still gets an entry and its stock credited back; zero-delta products never lock/read/write; `SELECT id FROM "Product" ... FOR UPDATE` locks the touched rows first (mirrors `create()`'s lock — the old guard read unlocked because it wrote nothing); CUSTOMER/DRIVER still hard-block 409 INSUFFICIENT_STOCK with NO write on a violation, staff still warn-only but the write now proceeds after the warn (may go negative); DRAFT orders skip validation+write+query entirely (internal guard, defense-in-depth for the call site that can't reach DRAFT anyway); no StockMovement rows either way, matching `create()`. Both call sites renamed (`updateOrderItems` ~:3445, `approveChangeRequestAtStop`'s at-door merge ~:4393) — the second one previously never touched `Product.currentStock` at all (comment claimed stock was `recordSale`-only at delivery; that's now WRONG, corrected in api.md). Specs: rewrote the one pre-existing test that asserted the old no-write behavior (that was the exact defect), added a regression pair pinning `create()`'s mixed pieces/selling-units decrement basis (a boxed product ordered as `{boxes,pieces}` decrements in PIECES, the same product ordered bare `qty` decrements in SELLING UNITS — intentionally NOT unified, just documented), and added 10 direct-call cases (a-j) against the private method itself (same pattern as the existing `linePieceQty` specs) covering increase/decrease/remove/add/DRAFT/non-staff-block/staff-oversell/no-op-zero-writes plus the two delivered-clamp cases below. **Review fixes applied in the same worktree:** (F1, money-critical) a negative delta credited the FULL line qty back even when the goods were already DELIVERED — invented inventory; settle now builds a parallel `deliveredHeld` map in the same pass as `held` (same non-CANCELLED filter, `Number(li.deliveredQty ?? 0)`) and caps every credit at `max(0, held − deliveredHeld)`, skipping the write entirely when the cap is 0 — the same call the at-door path makes by refusing `LINE_ALREADY_DELIVERED`; specs (i) fully-delivered removal writes nothing and (j) 24-with-10-delivered reduced to 0 credits exactly 14. (F2) `held` was derived from an `order.lineItems` snapshot read OUTSIDE the transaction — stale under a concurrent edit (web PATCH vs at-door approve) and therefore a wrong-MAGNITUDE, permanently wrong stock write; BOTH transactions (`updateOrderItems`, `approveChangeRequestAtStop`) now open with `SELECT id FROM "Order" WHERE id = … FOR UPDATE` and re-read the pre-edit lines in-tx (`tx.orderItem.findMany` selecting productId/qty/deliveredQty/status) before any orderItem write; settle's signature took a `heldItems` argument for it (`(db, order{id,status}, heldItems, finalActiveItems, user?)`) and keeps the DRAFT guard on `order.status`. (F3) each delta is rounded to the qty column's 3 decimals before the `!== 0` test (and the rounded value is written) so `Decimal(10,3)` float residue can't emit a phantom adjustment. (F4) the docblock's "delivery decrements separately via InventoryService.recordSale" was false (recordSale has had no caller since c5f579c2) — corrected to state that nothing else decrements for delivery. (EXTRA-1) new spec proving the AT-DOOR call site writes stock at all: an approved CHANGE_QTY increase 12→36 decrements 24 (that site never touched `currentStock` before WP1). **WP4-reduced (web, no dependency on WP1):** new `apps/web/lib/invoice-terms.ts` holding `getDaysForTerms`/`addDaysIso` extracted VERBATIM from `invoices/new/page.tsx` (which now imports them — DST-safe UTC arithmetic, mirrors mobile's `lib/invoice-terms.ts`); `lib/formatting.ts` gained `calendarDaysUntil(d)` (UTC due-day vs LOCAL today, negative = overdue) and `invoices/page.tsx` `renderStatus` swapped its two `setHours(0,0,0,0)` sites onto it (the KPI buckets were already fixed by #442); `EditTermsModal` in `invoices/[id]/page.tsx` now recomputes the due date from the invoice's ISSUE date whenever a known term is picked (the client-reported "Net 60 shows Net 30" — the select previously only set the label), with "Select terms…" leaving the date alone and the muted helper line rendered only when there IS an issueDate to anchor it. Full orders.service.spec.ts: 253/253 green; `tsc -p apps/api/tsconfig.build.json --noEmit` and web `tsc --noEmit` clean.
- **2026-08-26** — E2E SUITE RESURRECTION (worktree ecstatic-nash; no schema change): every master-push/nightly Playwright run had been red since 2026-08-21 while `global.setup.ts` swallowed a seed that ALWAYS failed in CI (no DATABASE_URL in either workflow → localhost fallback, and the e2e job has no DB service). The five failing specs were root-caused individually — none was actually caused by the missing seed: (1) REAL BUG `StockCountTab.tsx` — a fresh count session is created empty server-side but wasn't marked hydrated, so the session-detail query's `lines: []` wiped the first scanned row moments after it appeared (COUNT-01, red since #371 deployed); fixed by `hydratedSessionRef.current = res.id` on non-amend `beginNewSession` (amend sessions still hydrate — they ARE seeded server-side). (2) REAL BUG orders select-mode row checkbox — a click ON the box fired the input's onChange AND bubbled to the td's onClick, double-toggling to a net no-op (trip-builder-gate caught it); fixed with stopPropagation on the input's own onClick. (3) spec 11 never-sold — `/api/v1/analytics/product-sales/:id` was unmocked, the live API answers an empty history for the fake product id, and `SalesHistoryCard` rendered a SECOND "Never sold" → strict-mode violation; endpoint now mocked + the assertion scoped to the Demand card. (4) OP-18b — #417's boxed restock layout (Boxes/+Pieces/Cost per Box) made the positional `nth(0)/nth(1)` fills leave the required Cost empty, silently blocking the submit; now fills FIRST + LAST number inputs (correct in both layouts). (5) BSD-01, plus 04's BY-09..12 which silently self-skipped forever — `[class*='seller'], [class*='card'] button` never matched the portal DOM; `SellerCard` (both variants) now carries `data-testid="seller-card"`, and the specs match by testid / role+businessName. Running the fixed specs against a FULL LOCAL STACK surfaced three more issues CI's prod latency had been masking: (6) REAL BUG `lib/hooks/useUrlSearch.ts` — the debounced input→URL write used `router.replace`, which CANCELS an in-flight `router.push` (type a search, click a row within the debounce window → the row silently never opens; reproduced on the customers list); the write is now a shallow `window.history.replaceState` (Next ≥14.1 syncs usePathname/useSearchParams from it; Back-restore e2e 12 re-verified green). (7) REAL BUG `buyer/invite/[token]/page.tsx` — register→accept→portal is all soft navigation under one BuyerAuthProvider whose sellers list was seeded `[]` at registration and never refetched, so a freshly-linked buyer landed on "No sellers connected yet" until a hard reload; `handleAccept` now awaits `refreshSellers()` before redirecting. (8) spec 15's history-page assertion hit ANOTHER strict-mode duplicate (shell `<h1>` + PageHeader `<h2>` both "Stock Counts") — now `{ level: 2 }`. All five formerly-red specs verified PASSING end-to-end on the local stack (COUNT-01, OP-18b, never-sold, BSD-01, trip-builder-gate). Seeding contract rewritten (`apps/web/e2e/setup/global.setup.ts`): SKIP_E2E_SEED=true → skip; configured `E2E_SEED_DATABASE_URL`/`DATABASE_URL` → seed and THROW on failure (no more "Continuing despite seed error"); CI without the URL → loud explicit skip (deployed app's standing `e2e-routeflow` tenant assumed pre-seeded); local without the URL → seed the local-dev fallback, failure aborts. ci.yml + nightly.yml pass the new optional `E2E_SEED_DATABASE_URL` secret through to the Playwright step; `e2e-seed.js` now prints the WHOLE error (Prisma wraps connection failures in an Invalid-invocation whose `.message` is empty — CI logged a blank cause all week).
- **2026-08-25** — iOS CSV-SHARE FIX (stacked on fix/ios-share-activation, branch claude/quizzical-dirac-58e99b): `shareCsvWeb` had the sibling blind spot the PDF fix closed — a NotAllowedError from `navigator.share({files})` (iOS Safari activation not surviving the CSV fetch) fell through to a silently popup-blocked `window.open`. The per-url LRU fetch cache was extracted VERBATIM from share-pdf.ts into pure `lib/pending-cache.ts` (`createPendingCache<T>` — get/release, pending-reuse, rejection self-eviction identity-checked, LRU cap; pinned by `__tests__/pending-cache.test.ts`); `pdfFileCache` is now an instance of it (behavior unchanged) and `shareCsvWeb` gets its OWN instance + `classifyShareError` routing: retap → `offerRetapCsvShare()` "Share CSV" dialog re-entering with the same url (cached File shared synchronously under the fresh tap), dismissed → done, canShare-false/fetch-failure/real-failure → plain-url fallback exactly as before. `shareCsvText` untouched. Callers (RegulatedFilingsList, compliance/[id] prepare-filing onSuccess — the latter runs with NO activation left, the worst case) unchanged.
- **2026-08-25** — iOS INVOICE-SHARE FIX (branch fix/ios-share-activation, customer report): `navigator.share({files})` rejecting with NotAllowedError (iOS Safari transient activation not surviving the PDF fetch — confirmed against the Web Share spec + WebKit User Activation docs) was treated as a hard failure AND released the cached File, so the phone share always died with a toast while desktop (window.open fallback) worked. share-pdf.ts now routes it via the new pure `lib/share-error.ts` classifyShareError to the existing budget-expiry recovery: cache KEPT, retapHandled callers flip to "PDF ready — tap to share", others get offerRetapShare — the second tap shares the cached File synchronously under fresh activation. - **2026-08-25** — ORDER-DELIVERY / RECURRING-ROUTES ADDON SPLIT (worktree `adhoc-trips`, WP1-WP8; no schema change): order-delivery (ad-hoc trips + `/deliveries` history) and recurring-routes (standing route templates + `/routes` + `/dispatch`) are now two independent per-tenant addons — `RECURRING_ROUTES_ADDON = "recurring_routes"` / `ORDER_DELIVERY_ADDON = "order_delivery"` (`packages/types/index.ts`) — with `developer_mode` kept as the master switch that unlocks both (`useDeliveryAccess`/`useRoutesAccess` composition hooks, `devMode || <feature>`, mirrored web+mobile in `lib/api/addons.ts`; `useDeveloperMode` itself is byte-unchanged). Web: `(dashboard)/layout.tsx` splits the "Dispatch" nav group (routes access) from a new "Deliveries" group (delivery access, Drivers appended when Dispatch is hidden); trip builder + history moved from `routes/trips/` to new `app/(dashboard)/deliveries/` + `deliveries/new/`, with `/routes/trips*` left as thin redirect stubs (nav active-state + deep-link compat) and a narrow `RouteGuard` exemption so a delivery-only tenant reaches the redirect instead of bouncing to `/dashboard`; `routes/page.tsx` drops its Trips card, defaulting to `kind: SCHEDULED`. `findAllRoutes` (`routes.service.ts`) additionally returns `_count{runs,stops}` + a latest-run summary (Date/Driver/Stops/Status derivation) — additive, existing filtering/pagination/SCHEDULED-default specs pass unchanged. Mobile: `(operator)/_layout.tsx` chokepoint splits sections into `ROUTES_SECTIONS`/`DELIVERY_SECTIONS`/`EITHER_SECTIONS` (dispatch hub + drivers need either access); `dispatch.tsx`, `orders/index.tsx` Select action and the `trips/` screens (relabeled Deliveries/Plan delivery) repointed at the composed hooks. **Review fix:** the Dispatch TAB itself still gated on `useDeveloperMode()` in `components/OperatorTabBar.tsx`, which would have hidden the bar entry — and therefore every widened section — from a tenant holding only `recurring_routes` or only `order_delivery`; it now passes `routesAccess.enabled || deliveryAccess.enabled` into `visibleOperatorTabs` (param renamed `devMode` → `dispatchAccess`, pure filter and its spec branches unchanged), matching `_layout.tsx`'s `EITHER_SECTIONS` and staying default-hidden while the addons query is in flight. **Review fix 2:** every delivery detail target lives under the routes-gated `/routes` prefix — `/routes/:id` for a dispatched run (also where `deliveries/new` `router.replace`s after a successful dispatch) and `/routes/templates/:id` for a draft — so a tenant holding only `order_delivery` was bounced to `/dashboard` from every delivery it opened; `matchGatedPrefix()` now downgrades every `/routes` path outside `RECURRING_ROUTES_PATHS = {"/routes","/routes/create"}` to `need:"either"`, leaving the recurring-routes surfaces themselves routes-only. **Review fix 3:** `more.tsx`'s Routes/Fleet/Drivers rows and `home.tsx`'s DISPATCH-READINESS hero / "Routes today" section / Active-drivers-vs-Customers KPI swap were the only other operator entry points into those surfaces and were still `useDeveloperMode()`-only, so an addon-only tenant got an empty More hub and a routes-free Home even after the tab widened; they now read `routesAccess.enabled` (Routes, Fleet, hero, Routes-today — all recurring-routes surfaces, and the hero's Fleet button is routes-only so widening it would be a dead end) and `routesAccess.enabled || deliveryAccess.enabled` (Drivers row + the drivers KPI, matching `_layout.tsx`'s `EITHER_SECTIONS`). Drive mode in BOTH files deliberately keeps the RAW `useDeveloperMode()` — the `(driver)` app is still `developer_mode`-gated at `app/_layout.tsx:178`, so widening it would hand an addon-only operator a button that bounces straight back. `app/(driver)/**` untouched. Platform-admin `admin/tenants/[id]` `AVAILABLE_ADDONS` gained both keys; `demo-seed.js` grants both. e2e `20-trip-builder-gate.spec.ts` (renumbered 19→20) now asserts the union access flag across the new `/deliveries`/`/deliveries/new` URLs — CI-only, not part of the local verify gate.
- **2026-08-25** — CUSTOMER-FEEDBACK BATCH (worktree `feedback-batch`; migration `20260905000000_customer_deposit_default`, additive): address CRUD+primary (web+mobile — edit all fields, delete with inline confirm + server 409 when a `RouteStop`/`RouteRunStop` still references it, delete-the-primary promotes the oldest remaining; `CustomersController DELETE :id/addresses/:addrId`); `CustomerFormModal` edit mode drops its inline address inputs (was a silent-discard trap) and links to the Addresses tab instead, add mode unchanged; "+ New agent" inside the add-customer modal opens the existing `AgentFormModal` and selects the created agent without leaving the form; per-customer `defaultDepositPercent` auto-applies to every invoice GENERATED from an order (`resolveDefaultTerms` now also returns `customerDepositPercent` off the same customer row; explicit per-invoice deposits still win; a customer with no default is byte-identical to pre-change, spec-pinned); invoices `dueFrom`/`dueTo` list params power web's Due Today/Tomorrow/Next-7-Days chips (compose with the existing unpaid-`statuses` filter, not a new mechanism); DELIVERED orders lose their dead web Reopen button (server's transition map was always `DELIVERED: []` — mobile never had it — SUPERSEDED later the same day by phase 2 below, which made the transition legal and put the button back); carrier-shipment card/section now gated to `fulfillPath === "SHIP"` or existing tracking data, web+mobile, orders+invoices (invoice surfaces can't see `fulfillPath`, so they also keep the card on ORDERLESS invoices — an order-linked invoice inherits tracking from the order, a standalone one has nowhere else to record it). **Also backfilled** the PR #422 payment-terms-model map entries (`resolveDefaultTerms`, `paymentTermsLabel` invariant, deposit v1, narrow terms PATCH) — that PR shipped without touching `api.md`/`web.md`/`mobile.md` at all, only `CHANGELOG.md`/`_meta.json`; the recon flagged the gap, now closed under a dedicated api.md heading. **Review fixes:** (1) BLOCKER — the due chips' composed `statuses` array went out through `apps/web/lib/api-client.ts`, which had no `paramsSerializer`, so axios emitted `?statuses[]=SENT&…`; Express's `simple` query parser leaves that as the literal key `statuses[]` and the global ValidationPipe (`forbidNonWhitelisted`) 400s it — every due-chip click (and the now-clickable DUE TODAY tile) broke BOTH the list query and the CSV export. The web `apiClient` now sets `paramsSerializer: { indexes: null }` (repeated keys, which the DTO's `@Transform` already handles), exactly as mobile's `buyerApiClient` has since the buyer portal shipped; `statuses` is web's only array query param (`/trips/eligibility` sends a comma-joined string), so nothing else changes. Verified end-to-end against a real Nest 11 + Express 5 app mounting the real `ListInvoicesDto`: 400 before, 200 after. (2) The chips computed their window from `todayIso()` (UTC) while the DUE TODAY tile computed local midnight, so the two disagreed for part of every day in the Americas; both now anchor on a local `todayLocalIso()` (mirrors `dispatch/page.tsx`) and `PaymentSummaryBar`'s KPI math compares `dueDate.slice(0,10)` calendar-date strings — the same UTC calendar day the table renders via `fmtCalendarDate` — instead of local-midnight `Date`s. **Same-day addition — sale-integrity phase 2 (plan .claude/pipeline/plans/2026-08-25-sale-integrity-phase2.md), WP1-WP6 shipped:** (WP1, money-critical) orders.service.ts create()/updateOrderItems switched their boxes/pieces "is this box entry" check from non-null to POSITIVE ((item.boxes ?? 0) > 0 || (item.pieces ?? 0) > 0) -- the web New-sale screen sent boxes:0,pieces:0 for plain-qty lines, overwriting a real qty with 0 (live bug: qty 0.000 at full unitPrice, $0 order, ungeneratable invoice); a new hard invariant now throws BadRequestException on any resolved qty <= 0. Review fix: every one of those normalizeBoxesPieces calls now also passes the caller's qty through -- the helper only honours a boxes/pieces split when unitsPerBox > 1, so a unitsPerBox:1 product (the web sends boxes:1 for ANY truthy unitsPerBox) derived qty 0 and the new invariant turned the exact live bug into a hard-400 sale; with qty supplied the non-boxed branch keeps the operator's quantity and boxed products are untouched. (WP2, money-critical) invoices.service.ts createSplitInvoices now allocates order.discountAmount across sibling invoices exactly like its existing regular-tax allocator (proportional by subtotal, largest group absorbs the rounding remainder), fixing a bug where a fully-discounted order generated an invoice that ignored the discount and over-billed the full subtotal; zero-discount payloads stay byte-identical. (WP3) orders.service.ts changeStatus liberalized its transition map so every state may step back one stage -- PENDING to DRAFT and DELIVERED to CONFIRMED/PARTIALLY_DELIVERED (owner reversed BUG-ORD-01: Reopen Order is legal again); the new transitions require a reason and are staff-only for free (the pre-existing role gates run first), a DELIVERED demotion 409s when the order sits on a COMPLETED RouteRunStop (reopen the stop from the run instead -- that reverses stock and payments) and clears deliveredAt, and the DELIVERED branch side effects stay keyed on dto.status === DELIVERED so neither demotion re-invoices. deleteOrder(id, user?)/bulkDeleteOrders(ids, user?) made money rather than status the gate: staff delete any status, non-staff keep the old DRAFT/PENDING/CANCELLED allowlist, and two 409s precede the delete -- Return rows (the one restrict-linked child) and blocking external payments off cancelImpact (wallet money is still handed back, #341 preserved). (WP4) CreateSaleDto.deliveredOn replaces deliveredNow binary in createSale: a future date schedules the order (requestedDeliveryDate), a past/today date delivers it on that date via parseOrderDate (backdating stays staff-only). (WP5/WP6) web (orders/[id]/page.tsx Reopen Order + delete-any, invoices/new/page.tsx delivery-date picker sending deliveredOn) and mobile (order-actions.ts, order-status-flow.ts -- DELIVERED demotions, PENDING to DRAFT, canDeleteOrder always true) mirror the server exactly. See api.md orders/ and invoices/ sections, web.md/mobile.md 2026-08-25 phase-2 bullets.

- **2026-08-24** — AD-HOC ORDER TRIPS + FULFILLMENT MODE (branch/worktree `adhoc-trips`; dev-mode-gated, no merge/deploy from this pipeline): new `Route.kind RouteKind` (SCHEDULED/ADHOC, unforgeable via the public routes DTOs) + `Order.fulfillPath` (reuses the pre-existing `FulfillPath` enum, no backfill) + three nullable `Driver` home-base fields (migration `20260904000000_adhoc_trips_and_fulfillment`, additive). NEW api module `src/trips/` — `GET /trips/eligibility` + `POST /trips` (both OPERATOR-only, one shared `checkEligibility` predicate: eligible = PENDING/CONFIRMED/PARTIALLY_DELIVERED ∧ `fulfillPath:ROUTE` ∧ no active `routeRunStopId` ∧ ≥1 address; ineligible orders 409 the whole request) build ONE `kind:ADHOC` route (one stop per distinct customer via the shared `groupOrdersForTrip`) with ZERO order writes; `resolveOrigin` supports DRIVER/ADDRESS/TENANT origin tiers (geocode failure 400s before `route.create`, never a half-built route). `findAllRoutes`/`getCustomerRouteAssignments` default to `kind:SCHEDULED` so existing SCHEDULED-only surfaces see no change. the dispatch sweep in `createRun` now also pins `fulfillPath: ROUTE` (SHIP orders never attach to a run stop, on SCHEDULED routes as well as trips — behaviour-identical for existing data, which is all-ROUTE and never backfilled), and ADHOC `orderIds` adds ONLY `id: { in: ... }` on top (conditional-spread, deep-equal spec pin) — the money-risk tripwire for this feature. `PATCH /orders/:id/fulfill-path` (OPERATOR/TENANT_ADMIN) rejects OUT_FOR_DELIVERY/DELIVERED/CANCELLED and, for `→ SHIP` only, an order on an ACTIVE run (dispatched orders stay CONFIRMED, so status alone let a live-route order flip to "Shipped"; stale run links stay switchable); `changeStatus` gained one additive SHIP-labeling branch, DELIVERED invoicing path byte-unchanged and spec-pinned deep-equal to ROUTE. Web: bulkbar "Plan delivery trip" + `/routes/trips/new` builder (PICKING→Build→BUILT→Send, optimize best-effort) + `act-plan-trip` command, all gated on `useDeveloperMode()`; SHIP pill; fulfillment filter across all 6 `useUrlFilters` touchpoints; both order forms round-trip `fulfillPath` through their parked-draft payloads. Mobile: `DEV_MODE_SECTIONS`+`SECTION_TO_TAB` gained `trips`; Trips row on `dispatch.tsx`; nothing added to `more.tsx` or `DEV_MODE_SECTIONS.orders`; `statusActions` extracted to `lib/order-actions.ts(status, fulfillPath)` (ROUTE output byte-identical, spec-pinned); `lib/trip-grouping.ts` mirrors `packages/types/trip-grouping.ts` byte-for-byte with its own Jest test. Shared: `packages/types` gained `RouteKind` + `trip-grouping.ts` (pure `groupOrdersForTrip`, re-exported). Phase 0 findings (`docs/phase0-adhoc-trips-findings.md`): demo-tenant optimize 400 root-caused to `lat=null` demo addresses (Prisma seed bypasses geocode-on-create) + `GOOGLE_MAPS_API_KEY` apparently unset on prod — fixed via deterministic `demoAddressCoords()` in `demo-seed.js` (creates AND upsert-update branches, idempotent); route-PATCH "replace-all wipes stops" landmine confirmed absent from this feature's path (`updateRoute` = scalars only, stops go through separate guarded endpoints). e2e `19-trip-builder-gate.spec.ts` (own project) asserts both dev-mode branches, read-only. **Review fixes:** (1) BLOCKER — `trips.service.ts` imported `groupOrdersForTrip` from `@routeflow/types`, whose entry point is raw TS (`main:"./index.ts"`, no build); `nest build` emitted the bare `require("@routeflow/types")` and `node dist/main.js` crashed at STARTUP (whole API down, invisible to `tsc --noEmit`/ts-jest). Fixed by mirroring the helper to `apps/api/src/common/trip-grouping.ts` (same convention as `common/shipping.ts`) + a `no-runtime-workspace-imports.spec.ts` guard that fails on any non-spec API value-import of the package; verified `dist/` clean and `require('dist/trips/trips.service.js')` loads. (2) `POST /trips` now tenant-checks `dto.driverId` (was an unvalidated global FK; `resolveOrigin`'s origin driver already was). (3) The DRIVER origin was unreachable — nothing wrote the home-base columns; web `EditDriverModal` and mobile `(operator)/drivers/[id]/edit.tsx` each gained a "Home Base" section (line1/city/state/zip, prefilled by splitting the stored `homeAddress`, omitted from the payload when unset-and-untyped so an unrelated edit can't burn a geocode call), and both `lib/api/drivers.ts` clients gained `homeAddress` + the four `home*` write fields (web via a new `UpdateDriverInput`). Both builders additionally gate the DRIVER option on the picked driver's `homeLat`/`homeLng` instead of on a driver merely being selected — `TripOriginPicker` takes `hasDriverHome`/`driverHref`, mobile reads the coords off `AdminDriver` — so a driver with an empty home base disables the option with "{name} has no home base set" + a link to their profile rather than letting Build 400. (4) The web builder gated its DEFAULT tenant-depot origin on `routeSettings.depotLat/depotLng` — SystemConfig values written ONLY as a side effect of a successful geocode during optimize/trip-create, settable from no UI — so any tenant that had never optimized opened the builder with the Build CTA permanently disabled and a disabled reason ("Set a depot in Settings → Route") pointing at a control that does not exist; both clients now gate on `depotLat != null || depotAddress` (exactly what `resolveTenantDepot` can resolve) and the copy points at Settings → Business profile. (5) The BUILT review list rendered the pre-optimization `frozenGroups` with 1..n badges beside a map showing the OPTIMIZED order; it now renders `builtRoute.stops` by `stopNumber` (orderIds keyed by customer off the Build-time snapshot), while the Build/Send CTA counts stay on the frozen snapshot. (6) The dispatch toast claimed the frozen order count even though the sweep drops orders cancelled or re-dispatched since Build — `createRun` now sums its `updateMany` counts into `attachedOrderCount` on the returned run (spec-pinned) and the builder warns with the number left off. (7) An ADHOC route dispatched WITHOUT `orderIds` used to fall through to the un-narrowed sweep — and the trips lists linked never-dispatched drafts straight at the generic route-detail screens, whose Dispatch action sends no `orderIds`, so one "Dispatch Run" click attached every open order of each stop's customer instead of the picked ones; a draft trip stores no order linkage, so `createRun` now 400s that case before any writes (spec flipped) and web `routes/templates/[id]` + mobile `(operator)/routes/[id]` hide their Dispatch action for ADHOC, pointing back at the builder. (8) Both trip lists (web `routes/page.tsx` Trips card + `routes/trips/page.tsx`, mobile `(operator)/trips/index.tsx`) rendered Delete on EVERY row, and `deleteRoute` has no run-status check — it nulls the orders' run links, hard-deletes every `RouteRunStop` (POD photos, signature, arrival/completion times) and the runs, while `DeliveryBatch`/`DeliveryMutation.routeRunStopId` are `ON DELETE SET NULL`, so tidying up a COMPLETED trip silently destroyed the only delivery record its orders had; the affordance is now draft-only (`!run`) on all three lists and `deleteRoute` 400s an ADHOC route with an IN_PROGRESS or COMPLETED run (SCHEDULED routes untouched — their delete behaviour is byte-identical). End state: hook-verified branch + OPEN PR + owner runbook — no merge, no prod writes, from this pipeline. **Same-PR addition (owner-directed, post-pipeline):** DRIVER PAYMENTS OPT-IN — at-door money collection gated per tenant by the `driver_payments` TenantAddon (affa yes, bb-distro no): body-aware `routes/driver-payments.guard.ts` on complete-with-payment (403 only when `payment.amount > 0` without the addon — $0/on-account closes keep working everywhere, they carry the delivered-basis invoice reconcile), spec `routes.controller.security.spec.ts` (6 tests incl. guard-absent-on-plain-complete), `DRIVER_PAYMENTS_ADDON` in packages/types, mobile `useDriverPayments()` + payment.tsx collection-UI branch (+ stop-index/short-pick CTA relabels), demo-seed grants the addon. Phase 0 Google-Maps finding CORRECTED: prod key IS set; Google Cloud **billing** is disabled (REQUEST_DENIED) — owner re-enables billing, no env change.

RULE for future code: a tenant-scoped `findUnique` with an exclusive `select` MUST include `tenantId: true`, or use `findFirst`.

Web: new `lib/api/sales-agents.ts` (all types + ~20 hooks; money/rate fields typed `number | string` because Prisma `Decimal` serializes as a JSON string), `SALES_AGENTS_ADDON = "sales_agents"` in `lib/api/addons.ts`, two nav leaves spliced into the `navStructure` memo (never `OPERATOR_NAV`), `app/(dashboard)/sales-agents/` (list + detail + AgentFormModal + AssignCustomersModal), `app/(dashboard)/finance/commissions/` (list + detail + GenerateStatementModal + RecordPayoutModal), a "Sales Agent" card on the customer detail page + an add-mode-only agent select in `CustomerFormModal`, and a staff-gated per-order `commissionRatePct` override in `CreateOrderModal` (payload spread is `!= null` so `0` = exempt is SENT) / order detail / `OrderDraftPayload`. Mobile: read-only "Sales agent" row on the operator customer screen only. **THE GATING RULE:** every query hook takes `opts?: { enabled?: boolean }` and every page passes it — a gated GET that 403s trips the `api-client.ts` PLAN_GATE toast bridge, so one un-gated hook on a shared surface (customer page, order modal) would toast every unflagged tenant on every page load; full pages additionally gate their `LockedPage` on `useTenantAddons().isLoading` to avoid a lock-flash. **Money:** STORED amounts only — `drift` renders verbatim as "Unclaimed", never recomputed; the sole client arithmetic is the display-time `total − paid` remaining in the payout modal, which the server re-derives and caps in-tx. **The stale-409 contract:** approve 409s matching `"stale"` in the MESSAGE (the body carries no machine code and adding one was out of scope) show a banner whose one click voids then re-generates with the SAME agent/period and `router.replace`s onto the fresh statement — two requests, not one tx, so a crash between them leaves a VOID statement and no replacement (ledger still clean; recovery is the normal Generate button). e2e `18-sales-agents-gate.spec.ts` (project `sales-agents-gate`) is read-only and branches on the tenant's live addon state, green in BOTH.
