- **2026-08-23** — SALES AGENTS & COMMISSIONS ENGINE (2026-08-23, PR-C, branch feat/sales-agents-engine; INDEX.md row + api.md `sales-agents/` section). Ships DARK: flag.sales_agents is granted ONLY by the SALES_AGENTS addon (catalog v10, no plan grants it), so the engine returns before touching a commission table and both controllers 403 until an admin enables it per tenant. NEW apps/api/src/sales-agents/: commission-math.ts (pure) + commission-engine.service.ts + commission-reconciliation.service.ts (@Cron "30 \* \* \* \*") + sales-agents + commission-statements controllers/services, all in commissions.module.ts which exports ONLY CommissionEngineService (consumers import one-way; no forwardRef). Money (owner-locked): base = max(0, subtotal - discount), tax and shipping NEVER enter, computed ONCE per document; a CREDIT_NOTE application reduces the base by its PRE-TAX share (creditApplied x goods/total), not face value; payable releases pro-rata with cash and only status=PAID InvoicePayment rows count (the DRAFT-payment trap) - a deliberate divergence from recomputeStatus, which filters only != VOID. Ledger invariant: drift = payable - claimedAmount - SUM(adjustments); rows stay mutable until claimed, claims move only via statements, and drift < -eps appends exactly ONE self-limiting CommissionAdjustment - which is why backdating, clawbacks, reassignment and order edits are all one code path. Schema migration 20260901000000_add_sales_agents_commissions: 9 models + 6 enums + Order.commissionRatePct, with @@unique([tenantId, invoiceId, agentId]) as the double-accrual lock and a hand-appended partial unique index AgentAssignment_open_assignment_key (one OPEN assignment per customer; Prisma DSL cannot express it). ~18 one-line syncInvoiceCommissionSafe hooks across invoices/credit-notes/orders - the wrapper NEVER throws (a commission bug must not block a payment write) and the hourly cron heals what it swallows; revertInvoiceToDraft/reopenInvoice/writeOff were wrapped in a tenantTransaction for this, and OrdersService.deleteOrder's invoice cascade was a real bypass (it hard-deletes invoices without going through deleteInvoice). WATCH OUT: every TestingModule building InvoicesService/CreditNotesService/OrdersService now needs a CommissionEngineService mock or Nest DI fails at beforeEach. Onboarding IS backdating - enabling the addon creates no retroactive accruals; bulk-assign + a backdated rate + recomputeCommissionRange builds the history, so there is no backfill script by design. No web/mobile UI - that is PR-D. REVIEW FIXES (same PR): adjustment kind is picked by CAUSE, not by the forced-zero target - an un-issued invoice (void / revert-to-draft / reopen) emits CLAWBACK, which the old heuristic mislabelled RATE_CHANGE on every void; a resolved agent at rate 0 (rateSource NONE, or an explicit 0 = exempt order override) KEEPS its placeholder row - only rows that stopped EARNING are deleted - so those syncs are genuinely idempotent instead of delete/recreate churn; and a new accrual row is inserted after a findFirst re-check of the double-accrual lock rather than by catching P2002, because inside the caller's tx Postgres aborts the WHOLE transaction on a constraint violation and swallowing it would only defer the failure to COMMIT (the one thing the ...Safe wrapper cannot save a caller from).

- **2026-08-19** — D1 BOXED-SUBSTITUTION MONEY FIXES (2026-08-19, map entries WRITTEN; PR pending) - three silent overcharges on the order-edit path, api + web + mobile in ONE PR (the server already accepted the wider payload and an old server ignores an incoming unitPrice, so there is no client/server ordering hazard). B1: mobile edit-items' fresh case-add omitted boxSplit:true, so buildOrderItemDiff stripped boxes/pieces and the server billed BOX price x PIECE count (a $24 case billed $288) while the local preview still showed $24 - fixed at the producer AND belted in the diff, whose boxFields gate now keys on boxes/pieces DATA presence instead of the separately-maintained boolean. B2: the substitute payload carried only {id, substituteProductId, qty} on BOTH clients, so 24 pieces onto a 12-pack billed 24x the case price; the NEW pure mobile lib/substitute-line.ts buildSubstituteLine (not in the plan's file list - an extraction, reviewed and kept) re-denominates the inherited PIECE count against the SUBSTITUTE's unitsPerBox, and web's SubstitutePicker.onSelect does the same inline (EditItemState gained originalUnitsPerBox/originalBoxSplit so undoing a substitution restores the line's own denomination). B3: orders.service's substitute branch hardcoded unitPrice = product.pricePerUnit, discarding both an operator override and the customer's tier price - it now honors item.unitPrice for OPERATOR/TENANT_ADMIN only (DISCOUNTED below the substitute's list, MANUAL above, originalPrice = that list, overrideReason/overriddenBy set), and the incoming split is normalized against the substitute's box size and honored only when the substitute is case-packed (a loose substitute would otherwise collapse a whole-case line to qty 0). Web's downward-only override test widened to an absolute EPS diff (it silently dropped upsells) and both web save paths now share substituteDtoUpdate. No pricing.ts change, no migration. Entries updated: api.md orders/ (Substitution branch bullet), mobile.md (Incremental order-item edit row + Tests), web.md ((dashboard) Substituted-line payload bullet). fileCount +2 (mobile lib/substitute-line.ts + **tests**/substitute-line.test.ts).

- **2026-08-18** — PR-B TAX UNIT + TENANT-SAFE MAPPING (2026-08-18) - new apps/api/src/common/tax-rate.ts taxRateFractionFrom is the ONLY parser of SystemConfig settings.taxRate: the stored value is a PERCENT and the helper returns a FRACTION (clamped 0-100 before dividing, so a stored "150" is inert without a backfill). orders.service.getTaxRate stopped treating that string as an already-divided fraction (a tenant setting of "5" would have taxed 500x once a mobile save routed through orders.create); call sites and money expectations unchanged, the spec mock just moved from "0.1" to "10". order-templates.service.generateOrder swapped ConfigService for SystemConfigService and now reads the tenant value per-request - it was taxing standing orders at a flat env TAX_RATE ?? 0.1, so AppConfig.taxRate/env TAX_RATE lost its last reader and was removed from configuration.ts. PATCH /settings now rejects a taxRate outside 0-100 (400), which is how "150" got stored past the web form. vendor-bills saveProductMapping is no longer an upsert: ProductMapping's unique key has no tenantId, so it is global - findFirst through forTenant then update-by-id or create, swallowing P2002 on the create only. No migration, no client change.
- **2026-08-18** — REVIEWED 2026-08-18 - no map change needed: the four apps/api/scripts/\_tmp-\*.mjs incident scripts (db-state, list-backups, restore-prod, tax-audit) were deliberately never mapped and have now been DELETED, the recovery being complete and verified. The durable recovery asset is apps/db-backup/RESTORE.md, indexed from INDEX.md in #355. A restore script is intentionally NOT kept in-tree: it necessarily DROPs the public schema, which is not something to leave lying around untracked. Kept instead: the verified dump at backups/ (gitignored) and the R2 bucket's 30-day series.
- **2026-08-17** — PROD DB RECOVERY (2026-08-17) - INDEX gains a 'DB backups / restoring production' row: the apps/db-backup cron service (nightly pg_dump -> Cloudflare R2) and its RESTORE.md runbook were not findable from INDEX during a real incident. Recorded there: restore with psql, NEVER a raw pg client - a plain pg_dump's `COPY ... FROM stdin;` / `\.` blocks are psql directives the wire protocol rejects, so a pg-client restore parse-errors (and will have already dropped the schema if it does that first). The two apps/api/scripts/\_tmp-\*.mjs files in the tree are one-off incident scripts, deliberately NOT mapped and to be deleted once the restore completes.

- **2026-08-17** — MOBILE UX BATCH PR-1 (2026-08-17) - two guaranteed-400 fixes plus two more of the same class found by the deep dive. New lib/order-status-flow.ts (ORDER_STATUS_TRANSITIONS / canTransitionOrder / demotionRequiresReason / canDeleteOrder) and components/ReasonSheet.tsx; canSendInvoiceNow added to lib/invoices-logic.ts. Fixes: Send was offered on a pending-mirror invoice (canSend read DRAFT alone, computed ABOVE pendingMirror); order demotions sent no reason; the Delete order tile rendered on every status though the server allows only DRAFT/PENDING/CANCELLED; and useReopenOrder's docstring claimed a DELIVERED order could be demoted to CONFIRMED (DELIVERED is terminal). Specs **tests**/order-status-flow.test.ts + extended invoice-actions.test.ts.
- **2026-08-17** — PAGE POSITION SURVIVES BACK (2026-08-17) - new lib/hooks/useUrlPage.ts is the page-number half of the Back-nav fix whose search half shipped as useUrlSearch in #346. All 12 dashboard list pages held `page` in component-local useState(1), so opening a row unmounted the list and Back re-initialised to page 1 (owner-reported on the mobile products list; identical on desktop). useUrlPage derives the value from ?page= and writes with router.replace; page 1 = absence of the param, matching useUrlSearch/useUrlFilters which already delete('page') on a re-ranking change. Each call site also needs its 'reset to page 1 on filter change' effect to skip its FIRST run (didMountRef) or the mount-time reset strips the restored ?page=. All 12 list pages converted. useResetPageOnChange (same file) replaces each page's filter-reset effect for the 4 pages that had one, comparing serialized values rather than a didMountRef so it survives StrictMode's double-invoked effects. Specs PB-01/PB-02 added to e2e/12-search-back-nav.spec.ts. An adversarial review of the change added three more pieces: useClampPage (out-of-range page pulled back into range once meta resolves - a URL-backed page can outlive its data and rendered a false 'no records yet' with an unusable pager); useUrlFilters.setFilters (batch write - two setFilter calls in a row do NOT both apply because router.replace commits async, which is also why a setPage(1) must never sit beside setFilter/setSearch: it rebuilds from the pre-filter URL and reverts the filter just picked, a HIGH regression fixed on invoices); and DraftDock/CommandPalette now merge intent params onto the current query instead of building a fresh one, which had been discarding ?page= and ?search=.
- **2026-08-15** — DB-SAFETY TRIO MAPPED (2026-08-15) — first map entries for prisma/seed.ts (multi-tenant aware test-tenant seed; assertTestTenant + new assertSafeTarget local-host guard w/ SEED_ALLOW_REMOTE=1 override — the guard is an UNCOMMITTED working-tree change on top of 945c8444), prisma/rls.sql (RLS policies, dollar-quoting repaired in 23a91c7d), and scripts/prod-readonly-audit.mjs (read-only prod audit, new in 613fce76).

Wired into 13 list surfaces (products, inventory, customers, suppliers, drivers, orders, invoices, estimates, credit-notes, returns, shipments, finance/payments, finance/expenses); modal/picker searches deliberately stay local. NOTE: CI does NOT run next build, so a Next-14 prerender error would only surface at Railway deploy; judged safe because useUrlFilters already calls useSearchParams indirectly on 5 shipping pages in the same route group.

- **2026-08-07** — REVIEW FIXES on the 2026-08-07 batch (11 confirmed findings, 12 refuted): (a) HIGH - a backdated order stayed auto-merge eligible, so the hourly sweep could hard-delete it (losing orderDate + order number and re-billing those goods today) or let it absorb another day items; orderDate now forces skipAutoMerge and the controller rejects mergeChoice=merge + orderDate (400, not 409, so the web merge modal does not re-open). (b) exact-NUMBER dedup lookups no longer hard-filter on supplier (NULL-supplier bills are routine from batch import + mobile name matching and were unreachable). (c) only matchedBy===number may BLOCK a create - fuzzy hard-blocking made manual bill entry impossible; batch adoption likewise requires number or it would post against a different document and discard the reviewed lines. (d) setCheckStatus CLEARED no longer fabricates settledAt=now, which was restating closed cash-basis periods. (e) the backfill script cast VendorBill.id (TEXT) to ::uuid so every --execute batch threw; dry-run returned early so it would only have failed against prod. (f) web: orderDate now survives draft park/resume and the merge prompt disables Merge when a date is set; the manual bill modal renders the duplicate banner instead of swallowing the 409. (g) mobile: Add-unlisted-item and web manual-code entry were unreachable after the declutter - both re-opened via testable predicates lib/unlisted-affordance.ts + lib/scan-fallback.ts.

(1) Duplicate purchase-invoice detection: VendorBill.supplierInvoiceNumber (normalized, NOT unique by design) + layered findVendorBillDuplicate (column -> legacy notes regex -> numberless fuzzy, VOID always excluded, items never compared) in its own duplicate-match.module.ts so VendorBillsModule avoids an ImportModule cycle; create() throws 409 DUPLICATE_VENDOR_BILL unless allowDuplicate, POST /vendor-bills/check-duplicate is the pre-flight, DRAFT match = resumable; batch postBatch now recovers from that 409 (attach + receive-if-draft) closing the create-then-crash double-create window; backfill script parses the number out of notes. (2) Backdated orders: Order.orderDate (staff-only, not future via END-of-UTC-day bound, <=2y past) drives deliveredAt on createSale AND changeStatus->DELIVERED (previously never stamped), and every from-order invoice path now derives issueDate = orderDate ?? now with dueDate anchored to issueDate (was anchored to now) -- regulated soldAt/periodBucket follows into the past month (owner-accepted restatement); resyncInvoiceLedger reversals still book in the CURRENT period. (3) InvoicePayment.settledAt = when money landed (may be FUTURE for post-dated checks), distinct from server-stamped clearedAt; cash-flow money-in + Payments Received switched to settledDateFilter (settledAt ?? paidAt) while P&L/AR-aging/statements/Time-to-Get-Paid deliberately did NOT. (6) empty-state said 'Drop invoices here' with click-only wiring -> added onDragOver/onDragLeave/onDrop mirroring ScanInvoiceModal's dropzone pattern. deriveStatus centralizes the CLEAN/NEEDS_REVIEW decision (unmatchedLines>0 OR supplierUnresolved OR lowConfidence -> NEEDS_REVIEW), used by both classifyAndPersist and updateItemLines. - **2026-07-10** — ORDER↔INVOICE DIVERGENCE FIX (LIVE — PR #181 e07be81 merged + deployed 2026-07-10, migration 20260714000000_order_invoice_units_snapshot APPLIED to prod [post-deploy divergence probe green]): the reported prod bug where an invoice line showed the per-BOX price as the whole line total (a live invoice billed the per-box price instead of the order's full line total). ROOT CAUSE: invoices.service.ts buildInvoiceItemData RE-DERIVED each line's box/piece split + subtotal from the billed qty and the LIVE product.unitsPerBox instead of copying the order line's stored money — so a packaging change after order creation re-priced the invoice, and selling-unit lines (boxes=null) collapsed to 1 box. FIX = copy-don't-recompute: buildInvoiceItemData(li,billQty,tenantId,{priorBilledQty?}) now copies li.subtotal (full bill) or prorates it via TELESCOPING cumulative rounding keyed on invoicedQty (partials sum exactly; kills the 24×1.46=35.04-vs-35.00 drift); derives a split ONLY when li.boxes!=null, else keeps boxes=null; stamps orderItemId + snapshot unitsPerBox. recomputeOrderFromInvoices re-keyed on orderItemId provenance (productId fallback) + preserves each order line's denomination (no phantom splits). completeStop per-batch delivery invoicing copies stored money the same way. orders.service updateOrderItems: qty-only edit of a box-split line now re-derives the split + prorates the box price (was leaving stale boxes → reverse-divergence); auto-reverts a SENT pending-mirror invoice to DRAFT on order edit (revertLinkedInvoicesForOrderEdit — blocks if the invoice has payments); createOrder feeds the QTY_BREAK promo gate the PIECE count. order-templates line math routes through computeLineSubtotal+roundMoney. void→unvoid now restores invoicedQty (releaseInvoicedQty matched by orderItemId). SCHEMA (migration 20260714000000_order_invoice_units_snapshot, git add -f'd, ADDITIVE, PENDING prod): OrderItem.unitsPerBox, InvoiceItem.unitsPerBox + InvoiceItem.orderItemId (SetNull, indexed) + backfill upb WHERE boxes IS NOT NULL. WEB: invoice-edit page treats a line box-split only if stored boxes!=null + uses snapshot upb (was live → corrupted on save); order-edit + read-only line total use snapshot upb / stored subtotal; SplitInvoiceModal preview prorates stored subtotal; new app/(dashboard)/\_components/LinkedDocPreviewModal.tsx (OrderPreviewModal invoice→order, InvoicePreviewModal order→invoice, DivergenceNote) — floating popups w/ 'Preview' buttons both directions + amber divergence banner on both detail pages when a fully-invoiced order.total ≠ Σ non-void invoice totals. lib/api types: InvoiceItem+={unitsPerBox,orderItemId}, OrderItem+={unitsPerBox,subtotal}, Order+=invoices[]. MOBILE: SplitInvoiceScreen + operator split-invoice prorate stored subtotal; customer order-detail renders stored subtotal + box-count label; OrderItem/buyer line types += subtotal/boxes/pieces. ALSO (batched separately, unrelated): vendor-bills.recordPayment overpayment guard + roundMoney; import invoice CSV line-subtotal rounding. GUARD: scripts/post-deploy-check.mjs gained an order↔invoice divergence probe; NEW READ-ONLY apps/api/scripts/order-invoice-divergence-report.mjs (per-line order-vs-invoice comparison + payment status; run via `railway run --service postgres node ...`). PROD DATA: 5 divergent orders found on the live tenant (the reported one; a box-bug; a stale order-total; a price-drift; a 4¢-rounding case) — ALL invoices UNPAID. Repair DONE (verified 2026-07-10): the read-only report + repair dry-run against prod showed all 5 live-tenant orders ALREADY reconciled (0 divergent across active tenants; the reported invoice now matches its order; 2 invoices SENT / 3 DRAFT) — the divergences were fixed earlier (before #182 merged), so NO write was needed. The one-time repair script (added #182) was REMOVED once confirmed unnecessary; the read-only apps/api/scripts/order-invoice-divergence-report.mjs REMAINS for future spot-checks. Tests: buildInvoiceItemData regression (box-price-vs-line-total, rounding, telescoping, denomination preservation), qty-only reverse-divergence, auto-revert/block, unvoid invoicedQty restore, vendor-bills overpay; API 790+ green, web tsc+3 routes compile, mobile 143 green.
